- **maji-debug**: Disciplined debug session protocol. Read symptom → form hypothesis → verify before fixing → propose minimum-change fix. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-debug) · ★19)
- **maji-decision-log**: Keep an append-only decision log for a project. Use when a trade-off is settled in a session, when someone asks why something is the way it is, or before reopening a choice that was already made. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-decision-log) · ★19)
- **maji-doc**: Generate concise documentation from code. README, function docs, API references, architecture notes — written in plain language, with examples that work, no padding. Use when shipping or onboarding and you want docs that read fast and stay accurate. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-doc) · ★19)
- **maji-explain**: Concise code or concept explainer. Lead with one-sentence answer, then layered detail only if useful. No padding, no "let me walk you through". Use when encountering unfamiliar code, library, error message, or concept and you want fast comprehension instead of a textbook lecture. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-explain) · ★19)
- **maji-jimat**: Token-economy mode for any coding agent. Strip filler, cap verbose replies, and switch on graduated compression (ringan / penuh / ultra) or the sibling modes dry / answer-only. Fires when the user types "jimat on/off", "jimat ringan/penuh/ultra", "dry on/off", "answer-only on/off", or a per-message "/jimat" prefix. Preserves code, file paths, and accuracy. Also covers agentic token economy: extracting before fan-out, batching items per subagent, pipelines without barriers, and durable state, for when "the workflow burns too many tokens" or "we hit the usage limit". ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-jimat) · ★19)
- **maji-mode**: A Claude collaboration discipline. Lead with outcome+constraint, identify decision modes (discuss/build/pause/pivot), recognize frustration signals, enforce a Pre-Action Gate before risky operations. Use when starting any session to ship with less rework and scope drift. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-mode) · ★19)
- **maji-post-mortem**: Write a blameless post-mortem after something went wrong (failed deploy, broken tests, data loss, leaked secret, a dead-end approach that cost hours): timeline, impact, root cause, what helped, what hurt, and concrete action items with owners. Appends to a project log and reminds you of past lessons when work touches the same area again. Use when the user says "post-mortem", "what went wrong", "log this failure", or after an incident or a wasted afternoon. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-post-mortem) · ★19)
- **maji-refactor**: Scope-guarded refactoring. Define target → identify minimum diff → preserve behaviour → verify with tests. Pre-Action Gate prevents scope drift into unrelated improvements. Use when you want a targeted refactor that does not turn into a 50-file rewrite. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-refactor) · ★19)
- **maji-responsive-audit**: Audit and fix a web page or component for every screen size. Checks mobile-first layout, dynamic viewport units, safe-area insets, 44px touch targets, fluid type, container queries, responsive images, horizontal overflow and mobile form inputs at 320, 375, 768, 1024 and 1440 px, then reports findings by severity with file:line and the fix. Use when asked to "make this responsive", "fix the mobile layout", "check it on phone", or before shipping a new page. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-responsive-audit) · ★19)
- **maji-review**: Disciplined code review skill. Outputs structured feedback grouped by severity (critical/important/style/suggestion) with file:line references. No preamble, no padding, evidence-first. Use when reviewing a PR, diff, or file and you want focused actionable feedback instead of a wall of comments. ([Ijam18/MAJI-Skills](https://github.com/Ijam18/MAJI-Skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/maji-review) · ★19)
- **maji-setup**: First-run setup for a MAJI Skills agent folder. Interview the user in short rounds, write me/profile.md from profile.example.md, create me/overrides/ and me/learned.md, then report which skills now have what they need. Also updates an existing profile section by section. Use when the user says "run maji-setup", "set me up", "set up my agent", "update my profile", "my stack changed", "change my brand", or in Malay "jom setup", "setup agent aku", "kemaskini profile aku". - **maji-typescript-strict**: TypeScript type-safety discipline for agents editing .ts and .tsx files. - **major-agronomy**: 农学（agronomy）的学习路径与解题辅导：产量构成分析、施肥与灌溉计算、田间试验设计、栽培方案制定。 用品种、密度、播期、水肥这些可调参数，在气候与土壤约束下换产量与品质。 当用户提到农学、作物、栽培、作物学、大田作物、稻麦玉，或问产量三要素、施肥量、灌溉定额、品种区域试验时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/agronomy))
- **major-animal-medicine**: 动物医学（animal-medicine）的学习路径与解题辅导：从症状推到病因，再给出治疗方案与预防措施。 覆盖动物解剖、动物生理、兽医病理、兽医内科、兽医外科与传染病防控，训练按物种校正的临床推理。 当用户提到动医、兽医、小动物医学、预防兽医学、兽医病理学，或问病例分析、鉴别诊断、 手术与麻醉方案、群发疫情处置时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/animal-medicine))
- **major-arcana**: The 22 Major Arcana cards mapped to color palettes, lighting, gradients, and moods for UI. Use when a design needs a symbolic color system or atmosphere, or when a user names a card such as the Sun, the Moon, or the Hermit. ([Ormus-Solutions/LibreUIUX-Claude-Code](https://github.com/Ormus-Solutions/LibreUIUX-Claude-Code/tree/HEAD/plugins/archetypal-alchemy/skills/major-arcana) · ★126)
- **major-arcana**: The 22 Major Arcana cards of the Tarot represent archetypal life stages and cosmic forces. Each card carries distinct energetic signatures that translate into color palettes, lighting moods, and visual atmospheres for UI/UX design. ([HermeticOrmus/LibreUIUX-Claude-Code](https://github.com/HermeticOrmus/LibreUIUX-Claude-Code/tree/HEAD/plugins/archetypal-alchemy/skills/major-arcana) · ★104)
- **major-archaeology**: 考古学（archaeology）的学习路径与解题辅导：从层位与器物组合判断相对年代、据形制演变排出文化分期、 按规范判读与记录遗迹遗物。它训练的是用可复核的田野记录把物质遗存还原成人群行为与年代序列， 而不是凭器形猜年代。当用户提到考古、田野考古、文物、考古学通论、中国考古、探方与地层， 或问遗存判读、地层关系分析、年代序列判断、考古论述题时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/archaeology))
- **major-automation**: 自动化（automation）的学习路径与解题辅导：传递函数建模与方框图化简、稳定性判据、PID 整定与控制系统设计。 用反馈把被控对象的动态特性改造成按预期收敛的闭环，并用稳定性和误差说话。当用户提到自动化、控制、自控、控工、控制科学，或问传递函数推导、劳斯/奈奎斯特判稳、根轨迹、PID 参数整定、超调量与稳态误差时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/automation))
- **major-axis-image-alignment**: Use when segmented single-cell masks from multiplexed tissue images (e.g., MIRIAM's multiplexed immunofluorescence or MIBI datasets) must be orientation- and size-normalized prior to training or applying an autoencoder shape descriptor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/major-axis-image-alignment))
- **major-biology**: 生物科学（biology）的学习路径与解题辅导：机制解释、遗传计算、实验设计与图表判读。 覆盖普通生物学、细胞生物学、遗传学、生物化学、分子生物学，在分子—细胞—个体—群体多层尺度上找因果。 当用户提到生物、生科、生命科学、生化、分子、细胞、遗传，或问中心法则、孟德尔比例、Km 与 Vmax、PCR 电泳判读时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/biology))
- **major-broadcasting**: 播音与主持艺术（broadcasting）的学习路径与解题辅导：稿件播读的停连重音设计、 即兴评述、节目主持方案、语音与变调纠错，训练把文字与现场变成可信的声音。 当用户提到播音、主持、播主、配音、普通话语音、播音创作基础、新闻播音、 即兴口语表达，或问停连重音怎么标、即兴评述怎么搭、主持方案怎么写、 上声与"一/不"变调怎么改时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/broadcasting))
- **major-business-administration**: 工商管理（business-administration）的学习路径与解题辅导：约束下的取舍与方案论证、组织与人力设计、 报表与战略的交叉验证。这个专业训练的是在资源有限时做取舍，并为取舍负责。 当用户提到工商、企管、MBA、企业管理、管理学原理、组织行为学、市场营销、财务管理、战略管理， 或问案例分析题、战略分析题、组织设计题、财务比率计算题时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/business-administration))
- **major-change-autogit-guard**: Use when code or infra changes may be major. This skill prevents forgetting auto-backup and decision capture by enforcing milestone judge + git-score sequence. ([RYN6666999/meta-agent](https://github.com/RYN6666999/meta-agent/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/major-change-autogit-guard))
- **major-change-readiness**: Gate before a major commit or merge: all tests green, CI same as reviewers, README and WORK.md current, docs and threat model aligned when security-relevant. Triggers: big PR, release branch, before merge, ship it checklist, threat model update, all pytest pass, documentation drift, major refactor readiness. ([themyerman/ai-skills](https://github.com/themyerman/ai-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/ship-checklist))
- **major-chemical-engineering**: 化学工程与工艺（chemical-engineering）的学习路径与解题辅导：物料与能量衡算、 精馏与吸收计算、反应器设计与流程放大。训练的是把实验室反应放大到工业规模、 算清物料与热量。当用户提到化工、化工艺、化学工程、过程工程、化工原理、 化工热力学、化学反应工程，或问物料衡算、精馏塔板数、吸收塔高度、 CSTR 与 PFR 体积怎么算时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/chemical-engineering))
- **major-chemistry**: 化学（chemistry）的学习路径与解题辅导：结构推断、机理推断、滴定与热力学计算、实验设计。 训练的是用结构解释性质、用机理预测产物，并用谱图与守恒关系自证结论。 当用户提到化学、应化、化学生物学、物化、有机、无机、分析化学，或问反应机理、 谱图解析、滴定计算、ΔG 与平衡移动、合成路线时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/chemistry))
- **major-chinese-literature**: 汉语言文学（chinese-literature）的学习路径与解题辅导：作品分析与文本细读、 文学史论述与作家评价、古文翻译与字词训释。当用户提到中文、汉语言、 汉语言文学、文学、现汉、古汉、文论，或问这段诗怎么分析、 文学史大题怎么组织、文言文怎么翻译与训释、名词解释怎么背得准时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/chinese-literature))
- **major-civil-engineering**: 土木工程（civil-engineering）的学习路径与解题辅导：画内力图、算配筋、 验算地基沉降、编施工组织设计。训练在安全、造价、工期三重约束下把结构立起来， 并用现行规范把每个数字说清。当用户提到土木、建工、结构、道桥、工民建， 或问弯矩图怎么画、配筋怎么算、沉降怎么算、超静定怎么解时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/civil-engineering))
- **major-clinical-medicine**: 临床医学（clinical-medicine）的学习路径与解题辅导：从主诉推到诊断与处置，全程保留鉴别诊断。 覆盖系统解剖、生理、病理、药理、诊断学、内科学、外科学。当用户提到临床、医学、MBBS、 内外科、诊断学、病理，或问病例分析、鉴别诊断、机制解释时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/clinical-medicine))
- **major-computer-science**: 计算机科学与技术（computer-science）的学习路径与解题辅导：程序设计、数据结构、 算法复杂度、计算机组成、操作系统、计算机网络、数据库。当用户提到计科、计算机、 CS、数据结构、算法、操作系统、计网、组成原理、数据库，或问复杂度分析、 链表/树/图算法、进程调度、TCP 传输、SQL 优化、代码题时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/computer-science))
- **major-contract-by-date**: 按公告日期范围查询A股重大合同信息（market.ft.tech）。用户问重大合同、某日/某月重大合同、合同金额、签约方时使用。 ([FTShare-Lab/FTShare-skill](https://github.com/FTShare-Lab/FTShare-skill/tree/HEAD/ftshare-market-data/sub-skills/major-contract-by-date) · ★65)
- **major-contract-by-symbol**: 按股票代码查询重大合同历史（market.ft.tech）。用户问某股票的重大合同、某公司签了哪些大单、合同金额时使用。 ([FTShare-Lab/FTShare-skill](https://github.com/FTShare-Lab/FTShare-skill/tree/HEAD/ftshare-market-data/sub-skills/major-contract-by-symbol) · ★65)
- **major-contract-summary**: 近一年A股个股重大合同汇总排名（market.ft.tech）。用户问重大合同排行榜、哪些公司签合同最多/金额最大、合同总额排名时使用。 ([FTShare-Lab/FTShare-skill](https://github.com/FTShare-Lab/FTShare-skill/tree/HEAD/ftshare-market-data/sub-skills/major-contract-summary) · ★65)
- **major-deep-dive**: Full sync into Major — instructs the agent to immediately Read AGENTS.md, SPEC.md, docs/CONTEXT.md, docs/runbook.md, docs/failure-modes.md, and every ADR into context (~1,200 lines total). Use when about to do substantial Major work and would rather pay the upfront context cost than fetch each doc on demand. For lightweight orientation only, use major-dive. ([MioMarker/major](https://github.com/MioMarker/major/tree/HEAD/common/docs/agents/skills/major-deep-dive))
- **major-design**: 设计学（design）的学习路径与解题辅导：设计提案、用户研究、版式评析。 它训练在用户、工艺与预算的约束下做出可用且好看的方案，并用测试数据证明方案有效。 当用户提到设计、视传、视觉传达、环艺、工设、UI，或问设计提案怎么写、 用户调研怎么做、版式怎么评、方案迭代怎么说明时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/design))
- **major-dive**: Lightweight orientation map for Major — the AFK orchestrator at ~/Projects/major. ~76 lines: primitives, hard boundaries, file layout, and pointers to deeper docs. Use as the front door before any Major work; the agent reads referenced docs (SPEC, runbook, ADRs, failure-modes) lazily as questions arise. For a one-shot heavyweight load of all Major docs at once, use major-deep-dive instead. ([MioMarker/major](https://github.com/MioMarker/major/tree/HEAD/common/docs/agents/skills/major-dive))

- **major-economics**: 经济学（economics）的学习路径与解题辅导：约束下的最优化计算、供需与福利的图形分析、 回归结果的因果解读、宏观政策论述。它训练用模型解释选择、用数据检验因果，而不是背结论。 当用户提到经济、经济学类、ECON、微观、宏观、计量，或问效用最大化求解、 IS-LM 与乘数、回归系数解释、政策效果评估时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/economics))
- **major-education**: 教育学（education）的学习路径与解题辅导：教学设计、教育现象分析、教育研究设计、论述题写作。 训练用学习科学与测量证据解释“人怎样学会”，并把教学主张落成可验证的干预方案。 当用户提到教育、教育学类、教原、课程与教学论、教育心理学、教育研究方法， 或问怎么写教学目标、怎么分析教育现象、怎么做教育实验时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/education))
- **major-educational-technology**: 教育技术学（educational-technology）的学习路径与解题辅导：教学设计方案撰写、 媒体与技术选型论证、评价方案设计、技术方案可行性分析。 训练把学习目标翻译成可执行的教学设计与技术方案，并用证据证明有效。 当用户提到教技、教育技术、教学设计、信息化教学、LMS， 或问 ADDIE 怎么写、媒体怎么选、量规与可用性测试怎么做时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/educational-technology))
- **major-electrical-engineering**: 电气工程及其自动化（electrical-engineering）的学习路径与解题辅导：电路与电机计算、 潮流与短路计算、继电保护整定、系统稳定分析。训练的是在电压、电流、功率的约束下， 把电能从发电机送到负荷并保证故障能切除。当用户提到电气、电力、强电、电力系统、 电机学、电力电子、继电保护，或问三相功率、标幺值、潮流节点、短路电流、保护整定时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/electrical-engineering))
- **major-electronic-information**: 电子信息工程（electronic-information）的学习路径与解题辅导：电路列方程、 信号时频域变换、系统响应与链路预算。训练的是把「信号—电路—系统」 接成一条链，用 dB、带宽与信噪比判断链路能不能用。当用户提到电子、电信、信工、 EE、通信工程、模电、数电、信号与系统，或问节点电压法、傅里叶变换、采样混叠、 QAM 星座图、链路预算时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/electronic-information))
- **major-engineering-management**: 工程管理（engineering-management）的学习路径与解题辅导：经济评价与方案比选、 网络计划与关键路径、挣值与成本控制、合同变更与索赔。 训练把技术方案换算成钱、工期与合同责任，并让它按期按预算交付。 当用户提到工管、工程管理、造价、项目管理、工程经济学、合同管理， 或问净现值与内部收益率怎么选、关键路径怎么找、挣值怎么算时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/engineering-management))
- **major-english**: 英语（英语语言文学，english）的学习路径与解题辅导：语言学分析、文学 close reading、 翻译评析与实操、学术写作规范。训练在语言、文学、翻译三条线上做准确的中英转换。 当用户提到英专、英语语言文学、翻译、TESOL、英美文学、综合英语、英语语言学， 或问音位归纳与句法树、诗歌细读、译文评析、APA 与 MLA 引用时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/english))
- **major-ethics**: 伦理学（ethics）的学习路径与解题辅导：案例伦理分析、理论辨析、思想实验评析、 伦理决策报告。这个专业训练为具体情境中的「应该怎么做」建立可辩护的理由链。 当用户提到伦理、应用伦理、道德哲学、伦理思想史、道德心理学， 或问这样做对不对、义务论与后果论怎么选、知情同意与双重效应怎么用时使用。 ([cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student](https://github.com/cconlyprivate/study-skill-for-student/tree/HEAD/skills/major-learning-coach/majors/ethics))
- **major-feature-workflow**: Use when a change adds user-visible capability, changes public API, MCP contract, storage schema, module boundaries, indexing pipeline, or important dependencies.
